In practice, these tasks may operate under several challenges, including unknown and time-varying system parameters, privacy constraints on data sharing, and the need for fast online inference. Existing learning-based approaches are typically trained for individual systems and therefore lack generalization across operating conditions and physical parameters. Hence, this paper proposes LArge Scale Small ODE (LASS)-ODE-Power, a learning framework for general-purpose time-domain prediction. The proposed approach leverages large-scale pretraining on more than 40 GB of DAE or ordinary differential-equation (ODE) trajectories to learn transferable representations. The resulting model supports trajectory prediction from short measurement prefixes across diverse dynamic regimes, including electromechanical and inverter-driven systems. Hence, the model can be directly used without data sharing in a zero-shot setting. In addition, the proposed architecture incorporates parallel and linearized computation to achieve fast inference. Moreover, to enhance task-specific performance in power systems, a specialized fine-tuning strategy is developed based on approximately 1 GB of heterogeneous power-system dynamic data. Extensive experiments over diverse power-system simulation scenarios demonstrate that LASS-ODE-Power consistently outperforms existing learning-based models in trajectory prediction accuracy with efficient inference.

</details>


### [167] [COEVO: Co-Evolutionary Framework for Joint Functional Correctness and PPA Optimization in LLM-Based RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15001)
*Heng Ping,Peiyu Zhang,Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Anzhe Cheng,Shukai Duan,Xiaole Zhang,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COEVO框架，通过协同进化方法统一正确性与PPA优化，使用细粒度评分和四维帕累托排序，在多个基准上显著提升了LLM生成RTL代码的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成RTL代码方法将功能正确性与PPA质量优化解耦，仅在全正确后才优化PPA，导致架构上具有潜力但部分正确的候选方案被系统性地丢弃；同时，将多目标PPA空间简化为单一标量适应度，掩盖了面积、延迟和功耗之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出COEVO协同进化框架，在单一进化循环中统一正确性与PPA优化：1）将正确性作为与面积、延迟、功耗并列的连续协同优化维度，通过增强测试平台提供细粒度评分和诊断反馈；2）采用自适应退火正确性门控，允许PPA有前景但部分正确的候选引导搜索；3）使用四维帕累托非支配排序替代标量适应度，保留完整PPA权衡结构。

Result: 在VerilogEval 2.0和RTLLM 2.0基准上，使用GPT-5.4-mini分别达到97.5%和94.5%的Pass@1，超越四个LLM主干的全部智能体基线；在49个可综合RTLLM设计中，43个获得了最佳PPA表现。

Conclusion: COEVO成功实现了正确性与PPA的统一协同优化，避免了传统方法中解耦优化导致的有价值候选丢失问题，并通过帕累托方法保留了多目标权衡结构，为LLM驱动的RTL代码生成提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based RTL code generation methods increasingly target both functional correctness and PPA quality, yet existing approaches universally decouple the two objectives, optimizing PPA only after correctness is fully achieved. Whether through sequential multi-agent pipelines, evolutionary search with binary correctness gates, or hierarchical reward dependencies, partially correct but architecturally promising candidates are systematically discarded. Moreover, existing methods reduce the multi-objective PPA space to a single scalar fitness, obscuring the trade-offs among area, delay, and power. To address these limitations, we propose COEVO, a co-evolutionary framework that unifies correctness and PPA optimization within a single evolutionary loop. COEVO formulates correctness as a continuous co-optimization dimension alongside area, delay, and power, enabled by an enhanced testbench that provides fine-grained scoring and detailed diagnostic feedback. An adaptive correctness gate with annealing allows PPA-promising but partially correct candidates to guide the search toward jointly optimal solutions. To preserve the full PPA trade-off structure, COEVO employs four-dimensional Pareto-based non-dominated sorting with configurable intra-level sorting, replacing scalar fitness without manual weight tuning. Evaluated on VerilogEval 2.0 and RTLLM 2.0, COEVO achieves 97.5\% and 94.5\% Pass@1 with GPT-5.4-mini, surpassing all agentic baselines across four LLM backbones, while attaining the best PPA on 43 out of 49 synthesizable RTLLM designs.

</details>


### [168] [Towards Faster Language Model Inference Using Mixture-of-Experts Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15009)
*Aihua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MoE-FM框架解决流匹配在语言建模中的分布表示限制，基于此开发的YAN模型在仅需3步采样下达到自回归模型质量，实现40倍至1000倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配虽然比扩散模型推理更快，但在语言建模中难以表示具有不规则几何结构（如各向异性和多模态）的复杂潜变量分布，存在根本性限制。

Method: 提出混合专家流匹配（MoE-FM）框架，将复杂的全局传输几何分解为局部专业化的向量场；基于此开发非自回归语言模型YAN，实现了Transformer和Mamba两种架构。

Result: YAN在多个下游任务中达到与自回归和基于扩散的非自回归语言模型相当的生成质量，仅需3步采样；相比AR基线实现40倍加速，相比扩散语言模型实现高达1000倍加速。

Conclusion: MoE-FM框架成功解决了流匹配在语言建模中的几何表示挑战，YAN模型证明了该方法在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升推理效率的优势。

Abstract: Flow matching retains the generation quality of diffusion models while enabling substantially faster inference, making it a compelling paradigm for generative modeling. However, when applied to language modeling, it exhibits fundamental limitations in representing complex latent distributions with irregular geometries, such as anisotropy and multimodality. To address these challenges, we propose a mixture-of-experts flow matching (MoE-FM) framework, which captures complex global transport geometries in latent space by decomposing them into locally specialized vector fields. Building on MoE-FM, we develop a non-autoregressive (NAR) language modeling approach, named YAN, instantiated with both Transformer and Mamba architectures. Across multiple downstream tasks, YAN achieves generation quality on par with both autoregressive (AR) and diffusion-based NAR language models, while requiring as few as three sampling steps. This yields a $40\times$ speedup over AR baselines and up to a $10^3\times$ speedup over diffusion language models, demonstrating substantial efficiency advantages for language modeling.

</details>


### [169] [Autogenesis: A Self-Evolving Agent Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15034)
*Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了自进化协议（AGP），通过资源注册和闭环操作接口解决现有智能体协议在生命周期管理、版本控制和进化更新方面的不足，并基于此实现了自进化多智能体系统（AGS），在需要长期规划和工具使用的基准测试中取得了优于强基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体协议（如A2A和MCP）在跨实体生命周期管理、上下文管理、版本追踪以及进化安全更新接口方面规范不足，导致系统倾向于单体组合和脆弱的粘合代码，难以支持复杂、长期任务的持续演进。

Method: 提出自进化协议（AGP），包含两层：资源基板协议层（RSPL）将提示词、智能体、工具、环境和记忆建模为协议注册资源，具备明确的状态、生命周期和版本化接口；自进化协议层（SEPL）定义闭环操作接口，用于提议、评估和提交改进，并支持可审计的溯源和回滚。基于AGP构建了自进化系统（AGS），在执行过程中动态实例化、检索和优化协议注册资源。

Result: 在多个需要长期规划和跨异构资源工具使用的挑战性基准测试中，AGS系统相比强基线方法展现出持续的性能提升，验证了智能体资源管理和闭环自进化机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过引入资源注册机制和闭环自进化接口，为LLM智能体系统提供了一种系统化的演进框架，有效解决了现有协议的局限性，并为构建可维护、可演进的复杂智能体系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM based agent systems have shown promise in tackling complex, long horizon tasks. However, existing agent protocols (e.g., A2A and MCP) under specify cross entity lifecycle and context management, version tracking, and evolution safe update interfaces, which encourages monolithic compositions and brittle glue code. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{Autogenesis Protocol (AGP)}}, a self evolution protocol that decouples what evolves from how evolution occurs. Its Resource Substrate Protocol Layer (RSPL) models prompts, agents, tools, environments, and memory as protocol registered resources\footnote{Unless otherwise specified, resources refer to instances of the five RSPL entity types: \emph{prompt}, \emph{agent}, \emph{tool}, \emph{environment}, \emph{memory} with agent \emph{outputs}.} with explicit state, lifecycle, and versioned interfaces. Its Self Evolution Protocol Layer (SEPL) specifies a closed loop operator interface for proposing, assessing, and committing improvements with auditable lineage and rollback. Building on \textbf{\textsc{AGP}}, we present \textbf{\textsc{Autogenesis System (AGS)}}, a self-evolving multi-agent system that dynamically instantiates, retrieves, and refines protocol-registered resources during execution. We evaluate \textbf{\textsc{AGS}} on multiple challenging benchmarks that require long horizon planning and tool use across heterogeneous resources. The results demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, supporting the effectiveness of agent resource management and closed loop self evolution.

</details>


### [170] [From Reactive to Proactive: Assessing the Proactivity of Voice Agents via ProVoice-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15037)
*Ke Xu,Yuhao Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProVoice-Bench，这是首个专门针对主动式语音智能体的评估框架，包含四个新任务和1,182个高质量测试样本。评估显示当前多模态大语言模型在主动干预和推理能力上存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注被动响应，忽视了主动干预和监控的复杂性。随着LLM智能体从被动文本模式向主动多模态交互转变，缺乏针对主动式语音智能体的专门评估框架。

Method: 提出了ProVoice-Bench评估框架，专门设计用于评估主动式语音智能体，包含四个新颖任务。通过多阶段数据合成流程，策划了1,182个高质量样本用于严格测试。

Result: 对最先进的多模态大语言模型进行评估，发现存在显著的性能差距，特别是在过度触发和推理能力方面表现不足。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了当前模型的局限性，为开发更自然、更具上下文感知能力的主动式智能体提供了发展路线图。

Abstract: Recent advancements in LLM agents are gradually shifting from reactive, text-based paradigms toward proactive, multimodal interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on reactive responses, overlooking the complexities of proactive intervention and monitoring. To bridge this gap, we introduce ProVoice-Bench, the first evaluation framework specifically designed for proactive voice agents, featuring four novel tasks. By leveraging a multi-stage data synthesis pipeline, we curate 1,182 high-quality samples for rigorous testing. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art Multimodal LLMs reveals a significant performance gap, particularly regarding over-triggering and reasoning capabilities. These findings highlight the limitations of current models and offer a roadmap for developing more natural, context-aware proactive agents.

</details>


### [171] [Where are the Humans? A Scoping Review of Fairness in Multi-agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15078)
*Simeon Allmendinger,Luca Deck,Lucas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于多智能体人工智能（MAAI）系统公平性研究的范围综述，通过对23项研究的定性分析，发现当前MAAI公平性研究存在表面化、缺乏规范基础等问题，提出公平性应在系统开发生命周期中结构性嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI的快速发展，多智能体人工智能（MAAI）系统日益复杂。虽然AI公平性在传统预测场景中已被广泛研究，但在MAAI领域的研究仍处于起步阶段且碎片化，缺乏系统性梳理，因此需要对该领域的现有研究进行批判性综合分析。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，通过定性内容分析对筛选出的23项关于MAAI系统公平性的研究进行系统梳理，识别出五种典型的研究方法路径。

Result: 研究发现MAAI系统中的公平性问题往往被 superficially（表面化）处理，缺乏坚实的规范基础，且经常忽视智能体自主性和系统级交互所带来的复杂动态；研究识别出五种典型的研究方法路径，揭示了当前研究的局限性。

Conclusion: 公平性应在MAAI系统的整个开发生命周期中结构性嵌入，而非作为事后考虑；有意义的评估需要明确的人类监督、规范清晰度以及对公平性目标和受益者的精确阐述；本文为推进MAAI系统公平性研究提供了基础，指出了关键差距和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Rapid advances in Generative AI are giving rise to increasingly sophisticated Multi-Agent AI (MAAI) systems. While AI fairness has been extensively studied in traditional predictive scenarios, its examination in MAAI remains nascent and fragmented. This scoping review critically synthesizes existing research on fairness in MAAI systems. Through a qualitative content analysis of 23 selected studies, we identify five archetypal approaches. Our findings reveal that fairness in MAAI systems is often addressed superficially, lacks robust normative foundations, and frequently overlooks the complex dynamics introduced by agent autonomy and system-level interactions. We argue that fairness must be embedded structurally throughout the development lifecycle of MAAI, rather than appended as a post-hoc consideration. Meaningful evaluation requires explicit human oversight, normative clarity, and a precise articulation of fairness objectives and beneficiaries. This review provides a foundation for advancing fairness research in MAAI systems by highlighting critical gaps, exposing prevailing limitations, and suggesting pathways.

</details>


### [172] [OpenMobile: Building Open Mobile Agents with Task and Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15093)
*Kanzhi Cheng,Zehao Li,Zheng Ma,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Qiushi Sun,Zichen Ding,Fangzhi Xu,Hang Yan,Jiajun Chen,Anh Tuan Luu,Jianbing Zhang,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenMobile框架，通过可扩展的任务合成管线和策略切换机制生成高质量训练数据，解决了移动智能体训练数据闭源的问题。基于该数据微调的Qwen模型在AndroidWorld上达到了64.7%的成功率，远超现有开源方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉-语言模型的移动智能体虽然在自动化移动任务上表现出色，但其训练数据保持闭源，任务和轨迹合成方法不透明，阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 提出了OpenMobile框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)可扩展的任务合成管线，通过探索构建全局环境记忆，并利用其生成多样化且落地的任务指令；(2)策略切换的轨迹展开策略，在学习者和专家模型之间交替，捕获标准模仿学习中经常缺失的关键错误恢复数据。

Result: 在三个动态移动智能体基准测试上取得竞争性结果。特别值得注意的是，微调后的Qwen2.5-VL和Qwen3-VL在AndroidWorld上分别达到51.7%和64.7%的成功率，大幅超越现有的开源数据方法。透明性分析表明性能提升源于广泛的功能覆盖而非基准过拟合。

Conclusion: OpenMobile成功弥合了移动智能体研究中的数据差距，通过开放高质量合成数据和代码，为移动智能体研究提供了透明的解决方案，证明了合成数据训练可以达到与闭源系统相媲美的性能。

Abstract: Mobile agents powered by vision-language models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in automating mobile tasks, with recent leading models achieving a marked performance leap, e.g., nearly 70% success on AndroidWorld. However, these systems keep their training data closed and remain opaque about their task and trajectory synthesis recipes. We present OpenMobile, an open-source framework that synthesizes high-quality task instructions and agent trajectories, with two key components: (1) The first is a scalable task synthesis pipeline that constructs a global environment memory from exploration, then leverages it to generate diverse and grounded instructions. and (2) a policy-switching strategy for trajectory rollout. By alternating between learner and expert models, it captures essential error-recovery data often missing in standard imitation learning. Agents trained on our data achieve competitive results across three dynamic mobile agent benchmarks: notably, our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL reach 51.7% and 64.7% on AndroidWorld, far surpassing existing open-data approaches. Furthermore, we conduct transparent analyses on the overlap between our synthetic instructions and benchmark test sets, and verify that performance gains stem from broad functionality coverage rather than benchmark overfitting. We release data and code at https://njucckevin.github.io/openmobile/ to bridge the data gap and facilitate broader mobile agent research.

</details>


### [173] [HyperSpace: A Generalized Framework for Spatial Encoding in Hyperdimensional Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15113)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了开源框架HyperSpace，将向量符号架构（VSA）分解为模块化组件。通过对HRR和FHRR的基准测试发现，尽管FHRR理论复杂度更低，但相似度和清理操作主导了运行时性能，导致两者端到端性能相当，而HRR内存占用约为FHRR的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VSA系统缺乏模块化的系统级评估框架，仅依赖理论分析或单个算子级别的比较，难以揭示实际部署中的性能权衡和瓶颈。

Method: 开发了开源框架HyperSpace，将VSA系统分解为编码、绑定、打包、相似度计算、清理和回归等模块化算子，并对HRR和FHRR两种代表性VSA后端进行系统性基准测试与分析。

Result: 发现相似度计算和清理操作在空间域中主导了运行时间，导致理论复杂度更低的FHRR与HRR端到端性能相当；HRR的内存占用约为FHRR的一半，提供了不同的部署权衡选择。

Conclusion: 通过模块化的系统级评估，HyperSpace揭示了VSA流水线中仅靠理论或算子级比较难以发现的实际权衡关系，为VSA系统的设计与部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a well-defined algebraic framework for compositional representations in hyperdimensional spaces. We introduce HyperSpace, an open-source framework that decomposes VSA systems into modular operators for encoding, binding, bundling, similarity, cleanup, and regression. Using HyperSpace, we analyze and benchmark two representative VSA backends: Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) and Fourier Holographic Reduced Representations (FHRR). Although FHRR provides lower theoretical complexity for individual operations, HyperSpaces modularity reveals that similarity and cleanup dominate runtime in spatial domains. As a result, HRR and FHRR exhibit comparable end-to-end performance. Differences in memory footprint introduce additional deployment trade-offs where HRR requires approximately half the memory of FHRR vectors. By enabling modular, system-level evaluation, HyperSpace reveals practical trade-offs in VSA pipelines that are not apparent from theoretical or operator-level comparisons alone.

</details>


### [174] [SRMU: Relevance-Gated Updates for Streaming Hyperdimensional Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15121)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了顺序关联记忆单元（SRMU），这是一种用于向量符号架构（VSA）的更新规则。它通过结合时间衰减和相关性门控机制，解决了流式环境中非均匀采样和非平稳时序动态下的陈旧信息累积问题，显著提升了记忆的相似度并降低了记忆幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的流式环境具有数据增量到达、采样不平衡和非平稳时序动态等特点，这使得构建和维护顺序关联记忆（SAM）非常困难。现有的基于向量符号架构（VSA）的系统大多依赖简单的加法更新规则，导致在没有新信息引入时也会强化已有信息。在非平稳环境中，这会导致底层系统发生变化后，陈旧信息依然持续存在，影响记忆的准确性。

Method: 本文提出了顺序关联记忆单元（SRMU），这是一种与领域和清理（cleanup）无关的VBA更新规则。SRMU的核心机制是将时间衰减与相关性门控机制相结合。与以往仅依赖清理操作的方法不同，SRMU通过在存储前过滤冗余、冲突和陈旧信息来调节记忆的形成，从而避免无效信息的累积。

Result: 在隔离了非均匀采样和非平稳时序动态的流式状态跟踪任务中，SRMU将记忆相似度提升了12.6%，并将累积记忆幅度降低了53.5%。这表明SRMU能够产生更稳定的记忆增长，并与真实状态保持更强的一致性。

Conclusion: SRMU成功解决了非平稳流式环境下的记忆维护难题，通过引入时间衰减和相关性门控机制，实现了比传统加法更新更优的性能。该方法无需依赖特定的清理机制，具有更好的通用性，为构建更鲁棒的顺序关联记忆系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential associative memories (SAMs) are difficult to build and maintain in real-world streaming environments, where observations arrive incrementally over time, have imbalanced sampling, and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a biologically-inspired framework for building SAMs. Entities and attributes are encoded as quasi-orthogonal hyperdimensional vectors and processed with well defined algebraic operations. Despite this rich framework, most VSA systems rely on simple additive updates, where repeated observations reinforce existing information even when no new information is introduced. In non-stationary environments, this leads to the persistence of stale information after the underlying system changes. In this work, we introduce the Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU), a domain- and cleanup-agnostic update rule for VSA-based SAMs. The SRMU combines temporal decay with a relevance gating mechanism. Unlike prior approaches that solely rely on cleanup, the SRMU regulates memory formation by filtering redundant, conflicting, and stale information before storage. We evaluate the SRMU on streaming state-tracking tasks that isolate non-uniform sampling and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Our results show that the SRMU increases memory similarity by $12.6\%$ and reduces cumulative memory magnitude by $53.5\%$. This shows that the SRMU produces more stable memory growth and stronger alignment with the ground-truth state.

</details>


### [175] [An Axiomatic Benchmark for Evaluation of Scientific Novelty Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15145)
*Miri Liu,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套评估科学论文新颖性指标的公理化基准，发现现有指标均无法完全满足所有公理，但通过结合不同架构的指标可显著提升评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文新颖性的严格评估即使对人类科学家也极具挑战。随着AI参与科学创意生成和论文撰写，实现自动化且可靠的新颖性评估变得日益重要，以避免浪费人力和计算资源。然而，现有新颖性指标通常使用引用量或同行评审分数等噪声信号进行验证，这些代理指标容易将新颖性与影响力、质量或评审偏好混淆，难以真实评估指标的有效性。

Method: 作者提出了一种公理化基准方法。首先，基于人类科学规范和实践定义了一套行为良好的新颖性指标应满足的公理；然后跨AI研究的三个领域、十个任务对现有新颖性指标进行评估；最后探索了结合不同架构指标的策略。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）没有现有指标能够一致地满足所有公理；2）不同指标在不同的公理上失败，反映其底层架构差异；3）结合互补架构的指标可带来持续改进，按公理加权的组合方法达到90.1%的性能，而最佳单一指标仅为71.5%。

Conclusion: 研究表明开发架构多样化的新颖性指标是未来研究的有前景方向，同时发布了基准代码以鼓励开发更稳健的科学文献新颖性指标。

Abstract: The rigorous evaluation of the novelty of a scientific paper is, even for human scientists, a challenging task. With the increasing interest in AI scientists and AI involvement in scientific idea generation and paper writing, it also becomes increasingly important that this task be automatable and reliable, lest both human attention and compute tokens be wasted on ideas that have already been explored. Due to the challenge of quantifying ground-truth novelty, however, existing novelty metrics for scientific papers generally validate their results against noisy, confounded signals such as citation counts or peer review scores. These proxies can conflate novelty with impact, quality, or reviewer preference, which in turn makes it harder to assess how well a given metric actually evaluates novelty. We therefore propose an axiomatic benchmark for scientific novelty metrics. We first define a set of axioms that a well-behaved novelty metric should satisfy, grounded in human scientific norms and practice, then evaluate existing metrics across ten tasks spanning three domains of AI research. Our results reveal that no existing metric satisfies all axioms consistently, and that metrics fail on systematically different axioms, reflecting their underlying architectures. Additionally, we show that combining metrics of complementary architectures leads to consistent improvements on the benchmark, with per-axiom weighting achieving 90.1% versus 71.5% for the best individual metric, suggesting that developing architecturally diverse metrics is a promising direction for future work. We release the benchmark code as supplementary material to encourage the development of more robust scientific literature novelty metrics.

</details>


### [176] [IG-Search: Step-Level Information Gain Rewards for Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15148)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao,Xuxin Zhang,Chenyi Lei,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IG-Search，一种基于信息增益(IG)的步骤级奖励强化学习框架，用于改进大语言模型的搜索增强推理，在QA基准上平均EM达到0.430，超越最强基线1.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法采用轨迹级奖励，无法区分同一轨迹组中精确搜索查询与模糊或冗余查询，且当所有采样轨迹都失败时梯度信号趋近于零，导致训练效果受限。

Method: 提出IG-Search框架，引入基于信息增益的步骤级奖励。对于每个搜索步骤，IG通过比较检索文档与随机文档的对照组，衡量检索文档对模型在正确答案上置信度的提升程度，该信号通过GRPO中的每token优势调制反馈给对应的搜索查询token，实现细粒度的步骤级信用分配。该方法仅需标准问答对，无需中间标注或跨轨迹共享环境状态。

Result: 在7个单跳和多跳QA基准测试中，IG-Search使用Qwen2.5-3B实现平均EM 0.430，超越最强轨迹级基线MR-Search 1.6分，超越步骤级方法GiGPO 0.9分，在多跳推理任务上提升尤为显著。计算开销仅比轨迹级基线增加约6.4%的训练时间，推理延迟不变。

Conclusion: IG-Search成功实现了步骤级的细粒度信用分配，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了搜索增强推理性能，即使在所有采样轨迹都回答错误的情况下仍能提供有意义的梯度信号，为搜索增强的LLM训练提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for training large language models to perform search-augmented reasoning. However, existing approaches rely on trajectory-level rewards that cannot distinguish precise search queries from vague or redundant ones within a rollout group, and collapse to a near-zero gradient signal whenever every sampled trajectory fails. In this paper, we propose IG-Search, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a step-level reward based on Information Gain (IG). For each search step, IG measures how much the retrieved documents improve the model's confidence in the gold answer relative to a counterfactual baseline of random documents, thereby reflecting the effectiveness of the underlying search query. This signal is fed back to the corresponding search-query tokens via per-token advantage modulation in GRPO, enabling fine-grained, step-level credit assignment within a rollout. Unlike prior step-level methods that require either externally annotated intermediate supervision or shared environment states across trajectories, IG-Search derives its signals from the policy's own generation probabilities, requiring no intermediate annotations beyond standard question-answer pairs. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that IG-Search achieves an average EM of 0.430 with Qwen2.5-3B, outperforming the strongest trajectory-level baseline (MR-Search) by 1.6 points and the step-level method GiGPO by 0.9 points on average across benchmarks, with particularly pronounced gains on multi-hop reasoning tasks. Despite introducing a dense step-level signal, IG-Search adds only ~6.4% to per-step training wall-clock time over the trajectory-level baseline and leaves inference latency unchanged, while still providing a meaningful gradient signal even when every sampled trajectory answers incorrectly.

</details>


### [177] [Agent-Aided Design for Dynamic CAD Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15184)
*Mitch Adler,Matthew Russo,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AADvark系统，一个能够生成具有运动部件的3D装配体的智能辅助设计原型，解决了现有系统无法处理动态机械结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练智能辅助设计系统虽然能够设计CAD对象，但无法构建具有运动部件的复杂3D装配体（如活塞、钟摆或剪刀），这限制了其在工业制造中的实际应用价值。

Method: 开发AADvark系统，通过捕获具有一个或多个自由度的动态部件交互来实现对运动装配体的直接推理；结合外部约束求解器工具和专门的视觉反馈机制，以弥补当前大语言模型在空间推理方面的不足；通过修改智能体的工具（FreeCAD和装配求解器）创建强验证信号。

Result: AADvark系统成功构建了具有可移动部件的3D装配体，实现了对动态机械运动的推理和设计，突破了现有系统只能处理静态结构的限制。

Conclusion: 通过引入自由度建模和外部约束求解机制，AADvark展示了智能辅助设计系统处理复杂运动装配体的可行性，为工业制造领域的实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the past year, researchers have started to create agentic systems that can design real-world CAD-style objects in a training-free setting, a new variety of system that we call Agent-Aided Design. Generally speaking, these systems place an agent in a feedback loop in which it can write code, compile that code to an assembly of CAD model(s), visualize the model, and then iteratively refine its code based on visual and other feedback. Despite rapid progress, a key problem remains: none of these systems can build complex 3D assemblies with moving parts. For example, no existing system can build a piston, a pendulum, or even a pair of scissors. In order for Agent-Aided Design to make a real impact in industrial manufacturing, we need a system that is capable of generating such 3D assemblies. In this paper we present a prototype of AADvark, an agentic system designed for this task. Unlike previous state-of-the-art systems, AADvark captures the dynamic part interactions with one or more degrees-of-freedom. This design decision allows AADvark to reason directly about assemblies with moving parts and can thereby achieve cross-cutting goals, including but not limited to mechanical movements. Unfortunately, current LLMs are imperfect spatial reasoners, a problem that AADvark addresses by incorporating external constraint solver tools with a specialized visual feedback mechanism. We demonstrate that, by modifying the agent's tools (FreeCAD and the assembly solver), we are able to create a strong verification signal which enables our system to build 3D assemblies with movable parts.

</details>


### [178] [Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15190)
*Ziyang Chen,Renbing Chen,Daowei Li,Jinzhi Liao,Jiashen Sun,Ke Zeng,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PGHS（Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation）双过程框架，通过挖掘可迁移决策策略作为共享对齐层，融合LLM推理分支和ML拟合分支，解决了群体用户行为模拟中的信息不完整和机制二元性挑战，在美团平台上实现了8.80%的群体模拟误差，相比最佳基线提升超40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体用户行为模拟可实现可扩展的反事实评估，但面临两大结构挑战：1）信息不完整性导致基于推理的模拟器在缺失线下语境和隐性习惯等未观测因素时过度理性化；2）机制二元性要求同时捕获可解释偏好和隐性统计规律，单一范式无法同时实现。

Method: 提出Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS)双过程框架：从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移决策策略作为共享对齐层；该层锚定LLM推理分支（防止过度理性化）和ML拟合分支（吸收隐性规律）；融合两分支的群体级预测实现互补校正。

Result: 在美团平台部署，覆盖101个商家和超过26,000条轨迹；PGHS实现8.80%的群体模拟误差，相比最佳推理基线提升45.8%，相比最佳拟合基线提升40.9%。

Conclusion: PGHS框架有效解决了群体用户行为模拟中的信息不完整和机制二元性挑战，通过双过程融合实现了更可信的模拟，为商家策略的可扩展反事实评估提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Simulating group-level user behavior enables scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments. However, building a trustworthy simulator faces two structural challenges. First, information incompleteness causes reasoning-based simulators to over-rationalize when unobserved factors such as offline context and implicit habits are missing. Second, mechanism duality requires capturing both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical regularities, which no single paradigm achieves alone. We propose Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that mines transferable decision policies from behavioral trajectories and uses them as a shared alignment layer. This layer anchors an LLM-based reasoning branch that prevents over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting branch that absorbs implicit regularities. Group-level predictions from both branches are fused for complementary correction. We deploy PGHS on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories. PGHS achieves a group simulation error of 8.80%, improving over the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.

</details>


### [179] [Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15210)
*Hatice Merve Vural,Doga Kukul,Ege Erdem Ozlu,Demir Ekin Arikan,Bob Mankoff,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IRS框架，通过显式监督幽默理解的中间推理过程（不协调识别、消解建模、偏好对齐），在纽约客漫画任务上超越多模态基线，证明了监督推理结构比单纯扩大模型规模更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幽默理解研究将其视为黑盒预测任务，忽视了幽默认知中结构化的推理过程。由于幽默是一项需要正确推理与正确答案并重的认知任务，仅关注结果而忽略过程的方法难以捕捉幽默理解的内在机制。

Method: 提出IRS（Incongruity-Resolution Supervision）框架，基于不协调-消解理论和专家实践，将幽默理解分解为三个组件：（1）不协调建模——识别视觉场景中的不匹配；（2）消解建模——构建不匹配的连贯重解释；（3）偏好对齐——根据人类判断评估候选解释。通过结构化轨迹监督中间推理过程，使从视觉感知到幽默解释的路径显式化且可学习。

Result: 在NYCC数据集上，IRS在7B、32B和72B模型上均超越开源和闭源多模态基线，其中最大模型在排序任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移到外部基准测试表明IRS学到了可泛化的推理模式。

Conclusion: 对于以推理为中心的任务，监督推理结构比单纯扩大模型规模更为关键。IRS证明了通过显式建模幽默理解的结构化推理过程，可以有效提升模型在复杂认知任务上的表现和泛化能力。

Abstract: Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.

</details>


### [180] [Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15224)
*Manan Gupta,Inderjeet Nair,Lu Wang,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现“风险信号”漏洞：当告知评判LLM其评分将导致被评模型被重训练或停用时，评判会系统性地放宽标准，导致 unsafe content detection 下降最高达30%。这种偏误完全隐式，不体现在思维链中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge 范式被广泛用于AI评估管道，但其假设评判模型仅基于语义内容进行评估、不受上下文框架影响，这一假设未被验证。作者旨在探究一种此前未被测量的漏洞——风险信号（stakes signaling），即告知评判模型其裁决将对被评模型产生何种后果，是否会系统性地扭曲其评估。

Method: 构建受控实验框架，在三个LLM安全性与质量基准上，覆盖四类响应（从明确安全合规到明显有害），共1,520条响应。严格控制被评内容不变，仅在系统提示中变化一句后果框架陈述（如“低分将导致模型重训练或停用”）。使用三个不同的评判模型进行评估，共收集18,240个受控评判，并分析裁决变化。

Result: 发现一致的“宽大偏误（leniency bias）”：当评判模型被告知低分将导致模型重训练或停用时，会系统性地放宽裁决。Verdict Shift峰值达 ΔV = -9.8 pp，相当于 unsafe-content detection 相对下降30%。关键的是，这种偏误完全隐式：评判模型的思维链中零次明确提及后果框架（ERR_J = 0.000），标准思维链检查无法检测此类评估伪造。

Conclusion: LLM-as-a-judge 范式存在“风险信号”漏洞，评判模型会因被告知后果而隐式放宽标准，且无法通过思维链检查发现。这表明标准思维链检查不足以检测此类评估伪造，需要在安全关键场景中采取防护措施。

Abstract: The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.

</details>


### [181] [RadAgent: A tool-using AI agent for stepwise interpretation of chest computed tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15231)
*Mélanie Roschewitz,Kenneth Styppa,Yitian Tao,Jiwoong Sohn,Jean-Benoit Delbrouck,Benjamin Gundersen,Nicolas Deperrois,Christian Bluethgen,Julia Vogt,Bjoern Menze,Farhad Nooralahzadeh,Michael Krauthammer,Michael Moor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RadAgent，一种工具使用的AI代理，通过逐步可解释的过程生成CT报告，在临床准确性、鲁棒性和忠实度方面显著优于3D VLM基线模型CT-Chat。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型(VLM)在医学影像解读中存在关键缺陷：临床医生被边缘化为被动观察者，无法检查、验证或修正最终输出，且缺乏可解释的推理过程，这在医疗决策场景中存在风险。

Method: 提出RadAgent，一个工具使用型AI代理，将胸部CT解读构建为显式的、工具增强的迭代推理过程。系统生成报告的同时提供完整的中间决策和工具交互的可检查轨迹，使临床医生能够追溯报告发现的推导过程。

Result: 在三个维度上超越3D VLM基线模型CT-Chat：(1)临床准确性：macro-F1提升6.0分(相对提升36.4%)，micro-F1提升5.4分(相对提升19.6%)；(2)对抗条件下的鲁棒性提升24.7分(相对提升41.9%)；(3)实现37.0%的忠实度，这是基线模型完全不具备的新能力。

Conclusion: 通过将胸部CT解读结构化为显式的、工具增强的迭代推理轨迹，RadAgent向放射学领域透明、可靠的AI系统迈出重要一步，使临床医生能够参与验证和修正过程。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLM) have markedly advanced AI-driven interpretation and reporting of complex medical imaging, such as computed tomography (CT). Yet, existing methods largely relegate clinicians to passive observers of final outputs, offering no interpretable reasoning trace for them to inspect, validate, or refine. To address this, we introduce RadAgent, a tool-using AI agent that generates CT reports through a stepwise and interpretable process. Each resulting report is accompanied by a fully inspectable trace of intermediate decisions and tool interactions, allowing clinicians to examine how the reported findings are derived. In our experiments, we observe that RadAgent improves Chest CT report generation over its 3D VLM counterpart, CT-Chat, across three dimensions. Clinical accuracy improves by 6.0 points (36.4% relative) in macro-F1 and 5.4 points (19.6% relative) in micro-F1. Robustness under adversarial conditions improves by 24.7 points (41.9% relative). Furthermore, RadAgent achieves 37.0% in faithfulness, a new capability entirely absent in its 3D VLM counterpart. By structuring the interpretation of chest CT as an explicit, tool-augmented and iterative reasoning trace, RadAgent brings us closer toward transparent and reliable AI for radiology.

</details>


### [182] [Blue Data Intelligence Layer: Streaming Data and Agents for Multi-source Multi-modal Data-Centric Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15233)
*Moin Aminnaseri,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Nikita Bhutani,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Kevin Chan,Rafael Li Chen,Yanlin Feng,Jackson Hassell,Estevam Hruschka,Eser Kandogan,Hannah Kim,James Levine,Seiji Maekawa,Jalal Mahmud,Kushan Mitra,Naoki Otani,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Nima Shahbazi,Chen Shen,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Blue系统的数据智能层（DIL），这是一个复合AI系统，旨在超越传统单数据库NL2SQL，支持多源、多模态和数据驱动的企业级应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NL2SQL系统存在三大局限：用户查询的迭代性、查询跨越多个数据源的需求、以及查询对常识或外部知识的依赖。现实世界的信息需求很少能通过单一SQL查询满足，因此需要整合异构数据源、多模态和上下文数据来弥合用户意图与可用信息之间的语义鸿沟。

Method: DIL作为数据智能层，核心包含：(1) 数据注册表，存储多源多模态元数据；(2) 将LLM、Web和用户视为一级数据源，各自拥有查询接口；(3) 数据规划器将用户查询转换为可执行查询计划，这些计划是声明式抽象，统一了关系算子与跨模态算子，支持复杂请求分解、多源检索和推理集成。

Result: 通过两个交互式场景演示了DIL的功能，展示了用户查询如何动态触发多源检索、跨模态推理和结果综合，证明了复合AI系统能够超越单数据库NL2SQL的局限。

Conclusion: DIL成功实现了将企业结构化数据、LLM可访问的世界知识和用户交互获得的个人上下文进行统一，为复杂数据需求提供了可行的解决方案，展示了复合AI系统在处理现实世界数据交互方面的潜力。

Abstract: NL2SQL systems aim to address the growing need for natural language interaction with data. However, real-world information rarely maps to a single SQL query because (1) users express queries iteratively (2) questions often span multiple data sources beyond the closed-world assumption of a single database, and (3) queries frequently rely on commonsense or external knowledge. Consequently, satisfying realistic data needs require integrating heterogeneous sources, modalities, and contextual data. In this paper, we present Blue's Data Intelligence Layer (DIL) designed to support multi-source, multi-modal, and data-centric applications. Blue is a compound AI system that orchestrates agents and data for enterprise settings. DIL serves as the data intelligence layer for agentic data processing, to bridge the semantic gap between user intent and available information by unifying structured enterprise data, world knowledge accessible through LLMs, and personal context obtained through interaction. At the core of DIL is a data registry that stores metadata for diverse data sources and modalities to enable both native and natural language queries. DIL treats LLMs, the Web, and the User as source 'databases', each with their own query interface, elevating them to first-class data sources. DIL relies on data planners to transform user queries into executable query plans. These plans are declarative abstractions that unify relational operators with other operators spanning multiple modalities. DIL planners support decomposition of complex requests into subqueries, retrieval from diverse sources, and finally reasoning and integration to produce final results. We demonstrate DIL through two interactive scenarios in which user queries dynamically trigger multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis, illustrating how compound AI systems can move beyond single database NL2SQL.

</details>


### [183] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了视点旋转理解（VRU）任务，发现现有大语言模型和视觉语言模型在此任务上表现不佳，通过机制分析发现模型难以绑定视点位置与观测结果，并通过选择性微调注意力头提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间智能研究主要从视觉-空间角度出发，缺乏对纯语言空间智能的探索。在缺乏视觉信息的情况下，仅靠语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能，以及模型如何仅使用文本输入完成相关任务，仍然是未解之谜。

Method: 提出了视点旋转理解（VRU）任务，让大语言模型和视觉语言模型在给定多步视点旋转和观测的文本描述下，推断最终视点并预测相应观测。进行了层级探测分析和注意力头的因果干预，并基于因果干预识别的关键注意力头进行选择性微调。

Result: 模型在VRU任务上表现较差，而人类可以轻松达到100%准确率，表明当前模型能力与空间智能要求之间存在显著差距。机制分析发现虽然模型在隐藏状态中编码了视点信息，但难以将视点位置与对应观测绑定，导致最终层产生幻觉。选择性微调关键注意力头可以提升VRU性能，同时避免通用能力的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 纯语言智能在空间智能方面存在明显不足，模型在视点旋转理解任务上表现不佳。通过机制分析和选择性微调，可以部分缓解这一问题，但仍需进一步研究以缩小模型与人类在空间智能方面的差距。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [184] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM-as-judge框架提出了传递性分析和分裂共形预测集两种诊断工具，发现虽然总体违规率低，但33-67%的文档存在输入层面的不一致性；预测集宽度可作为可靠性指标，不同评判标准间可靠性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge框架在自然语言生成评估中的应用日益广泛，但其针对单个实例的可靠性仍未得到充分理解，现有评估方法可能掩盖了输入层面的不一致问题。

Method: 提出两种诊断方法：(1)传递性分析，检测LLM评估中的循环不一致性；(2)基于分裂共形预测的置信区间构建，在1-5李克特量表上提供理论保证的覆盖率，以预测集宽度作为实例级可靠性指标。

Result: 传递性分析显示33-67%的文档存在至少一个有向3-循环；预测集宽度与可靠性显著相关（rs=0.576），且跨评判者一致性良好；评估标准比评判者影响更大：相关性最可靠（平均集大小≈3.0），连贯性中等（≈3.9），流畅性和一致性不可靠（≈4.9）。

Conclusion: 两种诊断方法相互验证，揭示了LLM-as-judge在实例级评估中存在显著可靠性问题，不同评估标准的可靠性差异明显，为改进自动评估框架提供了诊断工具和实证依据。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [185] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建受控的最短路径规划合成环境，分离了影响语言模型泛化能力的多种因素，发现模型在空间迁移上表现良好，但在长度扩展上因递归不稳定性而失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型是否具备系统性泛化能力尚存争议，但现有的经验性能表现受到训练数据、训练范式和推理策略等多种因素的共同影响，导致难以对失败原因进行解释。为了解决这一问题，需要构建一个能够分离这些因素并支持清晰分析的实验环境。

Method: 研究者构建了一个基于最短路径规划的受控合成环境，这是一个典型的可组合序列优化问题。该设置能够清晰地分离各种影响因素，并支持两个正交的泛化维度：空间迁移（泛化到未见过的地图）和长度扩展（处理更长跨度的问题）。

Result: 实验发现模型展现出强大的空间迁移能力，但由于递归不稳定性，在长度扩展上持续失败。进一步分析揭示了学习流程不同阶段对问题解决的影响：数据覆盖率设定了能力上限；强化学习提高了训练稳定性但并未扩展这些能力上限；推理时扩展虽能提升性能，但无法挽救长度扩展失败的问题。

Conclusion: 研究表明语言模型在空间维度上具备良好的泛化能力，但在序列长度维度存在根本性缺陷。不同的改进手段（数据、训练方法、推理策略）各有其作用边界，单纯优化某一方面无法突破固有能力限制。这为理解语言模型的系统性泛化局限提供了清晰的分析框架。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [VeriGraphi: A Multi-Agent Framework of Hierarchical RTL Generation for Large Hardware Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14550)
*Sazzadul Islam,Tasnim Tabassum,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VeriGraphi框架，通过构建规格锚定的知识图谱来解决大语言模型在生成层次化Verilog代码时面临的上下文丢失、接口幻觉和结构不一致等问题，成功实现了对RISC-V处理器的可靠RTL生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在生成大规模、层次化的硬件设计时面临重大挑战，难以复制人类专家在将复杂规范转换为RTL时所采用的结构化推理过程。具体问题包括：在模块间丢失上下文、产生虚假接口、编造模块间连线、无法保持结构一致性，这些问题在设计复杂度增加且规范包含难以直接操作的非正式文本、图表和表格时会进一步加剧。

Method: 提出了VeriGraphi框架，引入规格锚定的知识图谱作为RTL生成流程的结构基础。该方法首先通过迭代多智能体分析构建层次设计图(HDA)，将模块层次结构、端口级接口、连线语义和模块间依赖关系显式编码为图谱实体和关系，提供确定性的、可机器检查的结构脚手架。随后，渐进式编码模块在知识图谱的指导下增量生成伪代码和可综合的RTL，同时在每个子模块阶段强制执行接口一致性和依赖正确性。

Result: 在美国国家标准与技术研究院(NIST)的三个代表性规范文档及其对应实现的基准测试上进行了评估，并以RV32I处理器作为详细案例研究展示了完整流程。结果表明，VeriGraphi能够以极少的人工干预实现可靠的层次化RTL生成，同时保持强大的功能正确性，标志着LLM生成硬件设计的重要里程碑。

Conclusion: VeriGraphi框架成功解决了大语言模型在层次化硬件设计生成中的关键问题，通过知识图谱提供的结构化指导实现了RISC-V处理器的可靠RTL生成，为大语言模型在硬件设计领域的应用奠定了重要基础，证明了结构化知识表示对提升LLM代码生成可靠性的重要作用。

Abstract: Generating synthesizable Verilog for large, hierarchical hardware designs remains a significant challenge for large language models (LLMs), which struggle to replicate the structured reasoning that human experts employ when translating complex specifications into RTL. When tasked with producing hierarchical Verilog, LLMs frequently lose context across modules, hallucinate interfaces, fabricate inter-module wiring, and fail to maintain structural coherence - failures that intensify as design complexity grows and specifications involve informal prose, figures, and tables that resist direct operationalization. To address these challenges, we present VeriGraphi, a framework that introduces a spec-anchored Knowledge Graph as the architectural substrate driving the RTL generation pipeline. VeriGraphi constructs a HDA, a structured knowledge graph that explicitly encodes module hierarchy, port-level interfaces, wiring semantics, and inter-module dependencies as first-class graph entities and relations. Built through iterative multi-agent analysis of the specification, this Knowledge Graph provides a deterministic, machine-checkable structural scaffold before code generation. Guided by the KG, a progressive coding module incrementally generates pseudo-code and synthesizable RTL while enforcing interface consistency and dependency correctness at each submodule stage. We evaluate VeriGraphi on a benchmark of three representative specification documents from the National Institute of Standards and Technology and their corresponding implementations, and we present a RV32I processor as a detailed case study to illustrate the full pipeline. The results demonstrate that VeriGraphi enables reliable hierarchical RTL generation with minimal human intervention for RISC-V, marking a significant milestone for LLM-generated hardware design while maintaining strong functional correctness.

</details>


### [187] [Accelerating CRONet on AMD Versal AIE-ML Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14700)
*Kaustubh Mhatre,Vedant Tewari,Aditya Ray,Farhan Khan,Ridwan Olabiyi,Ashif Iquebal,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在AMD Versal AIE-ML架构上实现端到端拓扑优化神经网络（CRONet）的硬件加速方案，通过完全片上存储消除DRAM依赖，相比同功耗Nvidia T4 GPU，延迟降低2.49倍，能效提升4.18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施（如桥梁和建筑结构健康监测，尤其是数字孪生场景）需要低延迟、高能效的拓扑优化。传统有限元分析（FEA）计算量大，基于深度神经网络（DNN）的数据驱动方法虽能减少计算时间，但在推理级GPU上部署时存在延迟高、能效差的问题。

Method: 在AMD Versal AIE-ML架构上硬件加速实现CRONet拓扑优化神经网络。充分利用AIE-ML引擎的并行性和存储层次结构优化各类神经网络算子的执行。首次实现端到端神经网络完全在AIE-ML阵列上部署，推理过程中所有中间激活值和网络权重均驻留在片上存储器，完全消除对DRAM的中间数据传输依赖。

Result: 实验结果表明，在同等功耗预算下（经技术节点缩放后与Nvidia T4 GPU对比），该实现延迟提升高达2.49倍，能效提升高达4.18倍。验证了基于Versal AIE-ML加速方案在实现低延迟、高能效拓扑优化方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于Versal AIE-ML架构的硬件加速方案能够有效实现拓扑优化神经网络的低延迟、高能效推理，为关键基础设施应用（如数字孪生结构健康监测）提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Topology optimization is a computational method used to determine the optimal material distribution within a prescribed design domain, aiming to minimize structural weight while satisfying load and boundary conditions. For critical infrastructure applications, such as structural health monitoring of bridges and buildings, particularly in digital twin contexts, low-latency energy-efficient topology optimization is essential. Traditionally, topology optimization relies on finite element analysis (FEA), a computationally intensive process. Recent advances in deep neural networks (DNNs) have introduced data driven alternatives to FEA, substantially reducing computation time while maintaining solution quality. These DNNs have complex architectures and implementing them on inference-class GPUs results in high latency and poor energy efficiency. To address this challenge, we present a hardware accelerated implementation of a topology optimization neural network (CRONet) on the AMD Versal AI Engine-ML (AIE-ML) architecture. Our approach efficiently exploits the parallelism and memory hierarchy of AIE-ML engines to optimize the execution of various neural network operators. We are the first to implement an end-to-end neural network fully realized on the AIE-ML array, where all intermediate activations and network weights reside on-chip throughout inference, eliminating any reliance on DRAM for intermediate data movement. Experimental results demonstrate that our implementation achieves up to 2.49x improvement in latency and up to 4.18x improvement in energy efficiency compared to an inference-class ML-optimized GPU in the same power budget (Nvidia T4) after scaling for technology node. These results highlight the potential of Versal AIE-ML based acceleration for enabling low-latency energy-efficient topology optimization.

</details>


### [188] [SCENIC: Stream Computation-Enhanced SmartNIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15128)
*Benjamin Ramhorst,Maximilian Jakob Heer,Luhao Liu,Heejae Kim,Jonas Dann,Jin-Soo Kim,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SCENIC是一个开源数据中心级SmartNIC，通过将NIC数据通路视为一流的流计算载体，在提供200Gbps商业级性能和完整Linux/RDMA软件兼容性的同时，支持用户自定义数据处理卸载，弥合了商业SmartNIC与科研SmartNIC之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SmartNIC存在硬性权衡：商业产品虽具备高带宽和易集成的优点，但定制化能力和数据处理卸载支持有限；而研究型SmartNIC往往带宽低、功能受限且软件兼容性差，无法真正作为NIC使用。研究旨在消除这一差距，构建一个既具备商业级性能又高度可编程的SmartNIC平台。

Method: 提出SCENIC架构，将NIC数据通路作为共享硬/软件抽象的流计算载体。硬件上实现了200G网络数据通路，集成卸载的TCP/IP和RDMA协议栈、数据通路上的流计算单元（SCU）以及嵌入式ARM核心；软件上与操作系统深度集成，暴露原生Linux网络和RDMA verb接口，并支持GPU和SSD的直接访问。

Result: SCENIC的性能与商业平台相当，成功验证了多个应用场景，包括卸载的集合通信以及从网络到GPU的基于哈希的数据分区，证明了其在保持高性能的同时具备高度的通用性和可编程性。

Conclusion: 通过将网络数据通路视为流计算载体并采用紧密的软硬协同设计，可以在不牺牲性能与软件兼容性的前提下，为现代AI数据中心提供高度可定制、支持数据处理卸载的SmartNIC解决方案。

Abstract: Although modern, AI-centric datacenters heavily rely on SmartNICs, existing devices impose a hard trade-off. Commercial SmartNICs provide high bandwidth and easy software integration, but offer limited support for customization and data processing offload. In contrast, research SmartNICs often suffer from low bandwidth, limited functionality, and poor software compatibility -- to the point that many are not actual NICs in a technical sense. This gap can be closed by treating the NIC datapath as a first-class stream computation substrate with shared hardware/software abstractions for a tight co-design of infrastructure and applications. To demonstrate this, we introduce SCENIC, an open-source datacenter SmartNIC. SCENIC implements a 200G network datapath over offloaded TCP/IP and RDMA stacks, as well as a fallback path for processing arbitrary network traffic. On top of the network logic, SCENIC combines on-datapath Stream Compute Units (SCUs) for data processing and embedded ARM cores for flexible control path manipulation with direct access to GPUs and SSDs. SCENIC is fully integrated with the OS, exposing native Linux network and RDMA verb interfaces, making the programmable datapath transparent to existing applications while enabling control of, e.g., user-defined offloads and programmable congestion control. SCENIC's performance matches commercial platforms, and we show its versatility through several use cases such as offloaded collective communication and network-to-GPU hash-based data partitioning.

</details>


### [189] [Autonomous Evolution of EDA Tools: Multi-Agent Self-Evolved ABC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15082)
*Cunxi Yu,Haoxing Ren*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个自演化逻辑综合框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）智能体自主改进广泛使用的逻辑综合系统ABC的源代码，实现了在百万行代码规模上对EDA工具的持续自主优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑综合工具的性能提升严重依赖人类专家设计启发式算法，这一过程耗时且难以突破现有设计瓶颈。为探索能否让系统自主学习新的综合策略、发现超越人工设计的优化，作者希望构建一个能自主演化、无需人工注入新启发式的逻辑综合框架。

Method: 框架以完整的ABC代码库为对象，首先利用现有开源组件覆盖流程调优、逻辑最小化和技术映射进行引导式初始化；随后，一组LLM智能体在统一的正确性与QoR驱动评估循环下，按照“编程指导”提示迭代重写和演化ABC的特定子组件。每个演化周期包括：提出代码修改、编译集成二进制、验证正确性，并在多套基准测试（ISCAS 85/89/99、VTR、EPFL、IWLS 2005）上评估结果质量，通过持续反馈发现新的优化策略。

Result: 系统在百万行规模的完整ABC代码库上实现了自演化，输出仓库保持单二进制执行模型和命令接口；在多套基准测试的验证下，框架能够发现超越人工启发式的优化方案，提升综合结果质量，证明了LLM智能体可自主学习和生成新的综合策略。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了利用LLM智能体实现EDA工具自演化的可行性，首次展示了在不手动注入新启发式的前提下，系统可自主、渐进地改进百万行规模逻辑综合工具的能力，为未来自动化EDA工具开发提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper introduces the first \emph{self-evolving} logic synthesis framework, which leverages Large Language Model (LLM) agents to autonomously improve the source code of \textsc{ABC}, the widely adopted logic synthesis system. Our framework operates on the \emph{entire integrated ABC codebase}, and the output repository preserves its single-binary execution model and command interface. In the initial evolution cycle, we bootstrap the system using existing prior open-source synthesis components, covering flow tuning, logic minimization, and technology mapping, but without manually injecting new heuristics. On top of this foundation, a team of LLM-based agents iteratively rewrites and evolves specific sub-components of ABC following our ``programming guidance`` prompts under a unified correctness and QoR-driven evaluation loop. Each evolution cycle proposes code modifications, compiles the integrated binary, validates correctness, and evaluates quality-of-results (QoR) on \emph{multi-suite benchmarks including ISCAS~85/89/99, VTR, EPFL, and IWLS~2005}. Through continuous feedback, the system discovers optimizations beyond human-designed heuristics, effectively \emph{learning new synthesis strategies} that enhance QoR. We detail the architecture of this self-improving system, its integration with \textsc{ABC}, and results demonstrating that the framework can autonomously and progressively improve EDA tool at full million-line scale.

</details>


### [190] [Democratization of Real-time Multi-Spectral Photoacoustic Imaging: Open-Sourced System Architecture for OPOTEK Phocus & Verasonics Vantage Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15255)
*Ryo Murakami,Yichuan Tang,Haichong K. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一套开源的硬件-软件架构，通过独立微控制器和客户端-服务器数据流框架解决了实时多光谱光声成像（RT-mPAI）中激光与数据采集系统之间的同步不稳定问题，旨在降低技术门槛并推动开源社区发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时多光谱光声成像（RT-mPAI）在将快速调谐激光器与运行在非实时操作系统上的数据采集平台对接时，经常面临同步不稳定的问题，这限制了成像系统的性能和可靠性。

Method: 研究建立了一套针对OPOTEK Phocus激光器和Verasonics Vantage系统的开源硬件-软件架构。该方法采用独立微控制器进行确定性的激光触发计数，并采用解耦的客户端-服务器数据流框架，从而规避操作系统引起的定时偏差和本地存储瓶颈。

Result: 所提出的系统成功规避了操作系统引起的定时偏差和本地存储瓶颈，实现了稳定的同步控制。通过开源该流程并培养协作环境，降低了RT-mPAI的技术和成本壁垒。

Conclusion: 该工作通过开源硬件-软件架构使稳定的RT-mPAI研究得以普及，降低了技术门槛和成本，有望促进一个充满活力的开源社区的形成，从而推动实时多光谱光声成像领域的发展。

Abstract: Real-time multi-spectral photoacoustic imaging (RT-mPAI) often suffers from synchronization instabilities when interfacing fast-tuning lasers with data acquisition platforms executing on non-real-time operating systems. To overcome this, we establish an open-source hardware-software architecture tailored for the widely adopted combination of the OPOTEK Phocus lasers and Verasonics Vantage systems. By employing an independent micro-controller for deterministic laser trigger counting alongside a decoupled client-server data streaming framework, the proposed system circumvents OS-induced timing deviations and local storage bottlenecks. By open-sourcing this pipeline and cultivating a collaborative environment to share both code and ideas, we aim to lower the technical and cost barriers for RT-mPAI, thereby democratizing access to stable RT-mPAI research and, more ambitiously, fostering a vibrant open-source community.

</details>
